Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [270] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合水印方法，在LLM生成文本的每个token中同时嵌入鲁棒信号（用于溯源）和脆弱信号（用于篡改检测），通过独立密钥和不同播种窗口实现双信号共存，支持三态检测（完整、篡改、无水印）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印虽然能抵抗编辑保持溯源能力，但这也让攻击者可以在保留归属的同时修改关键内容（piggyback spoofing漏洞）。需要一种既能溯源又能提供篡改证据的水印方案。

Method: 每个生成的token中同时嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号。两种信号共享相同机制但使用独立密钥和不同的规范化文本播种窗口，前者对编辑有韧性，后者对读者可见的修改敏感。通过多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保持期望生成分布，周期性轮次分配模式控制两信号间的权衡。检测时两个信号的评分构成二维空间，支持完整、篡改、无水印三种决策。

Result: 在两个大语言模型和两个提示数据集上，该方法在篡改检测率上优于所有评估方法，同时保持了有竞争力的溯源鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究表明，可靠的三态检测需要明确的完整性概念、双信号的联合嵌入以及对编辑的互补敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过联合嵌入鲁棒和脆弱信号，实现了在保持溯源鲁棒性的同时检测文本篡改，有效解决了piggyback spoofing漏洞，为LLM文本水印提供了更全面的保护方案。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [271] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIPES框架，利用语义先验与来源溯源信息来检测并防御针对工具使用型智能体的状态污染攻击，将攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体会从不同信任级别的外部来源消费数据，但工具响应很少标明每个组件的生产者及其应传达的含义。这一缺口使攻击者能够通过受控内容进行状态污染攻击，即攻击者内容超越了其响应组件的信息权责，对智能体的感知环境做出虚假断言，使后续行为在现有护栏看来是合理的。

Method: PIPES框架使用语义先验和来源溯源信息对响应单元进行筛查。当模式提供稳定期望时使用静态字段合约；对于开放式内容，则基于响应前轨迹和可信溯源元数据条件化筛查。PIPES标记违反语义先验或溯源层级关系的单元，部署时可选择移除、警告、阻止或升级处理这些违规单元。验证中采用原子移除实例化。

Result: 在三个VitaBench和三个AgentDyn数据集上，以Gemma 4 31B IT为目标智能体，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时良性效用保持较好（PIPES下92.5% vs 无防御时90.6%）。评估涵盖自适应PAIR风格攻击。

Conclusion: 基于溯源感知与语义先验的筛查机制能有效防御工具使用型智能体的状态污染攻击，在不牺牲良性任务性能的前提下显著提升安全性，为工具使用场景下的智能体防御提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [272] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealmEye是首个针对Arm CCA Realm VM的VMI系统，将完整的内省逻辑放置在RMM的R-EL2层，实现硬件强制的监控器与被监控VM分离，无需在Realm内运行任何代理，并通过硬件认证通道将结果返回远程所有者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密VM（CVM）的硬件隔离在防御恶意云提供商的同时，也使得VM所有者无法看到内部运行内容：通过网络或供应链攻击植入的内核rootkit可以在隔离保护下隐藏进程、篡改内核数据并窃取模型权重。现有的CVM-VMI系统（如00SEVen）依赖AMD SEV-SNP特有的VMPL0特权级别机制，该机制在Arm CCA上不存在，导致Realm VM没有任何可行的内省解决方案。

Method: RealmEye将全部内省逻辑放入Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2特权层。具体方法包括：(1) 直接读取Realm内存和寄存器，无需VM内代理；(2) 挂起VM以获取一致性快照；(3) 陷阱页面级访问；(4) 采用周期性自动触发模式，使扫描时序完全内部化于RMM，防止Hypervisor与Realm内rootkit串谋；(5) 通过硬件认证通道将结果返回远程所有者；(6) 提供CCA驱动后端，使LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具无需修改即可互操作。

Result: 在Arm FVP平台上，RealmEye成功检测了Diamorphine的进程隐藏和syscall表挂钩行为，其RMM内开销可通过原始调用次数线性预测。

Conclusion: RealmEye是首个在Arm CCA上实现完整VM内省的系统，通过将内省逻辑置于RMM层解决了Realm VM无内省解决方案的问题，且不依赖任何VM内接口，对现有VMI工具兼容。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [273] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码作为安全工件进行了首次大规模实证研究，在超过100万个PyPI制品中发现7,388个含字节码的制品（含228,578个.pyc文件），评估了反编译工具的分析能力、CPython运行时对对抗性字节码的鲁棒性，以及字节码发现无法从源码层面复现的问题。研究发现工具非稳健、存在17种鲁棒性签名、1,009个运行时发现（其中261组疑似内存破坏），且至少91.7%的发现能越过文档声明的非安全摄取边界执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全研究长期以源码为中心，但Python运行时可以直接执行.pyc文件、仅编译模块和marshalled代码对象，形成"检查-执行"鸿沟（inspection-execution gap），即安全审查审视的是源码，而实际运行时执行的是字节码，行为可能不一致。这构成了安全研究上的盲区。

Method: 研究采用多维实证方法：(1) 从PyPI收集1,034,843个制品并测量字节码暴露情况；(2) 使用版本感知的工具链评估字节码的可分析性（针对CPython 3.8-3.14）；(3) 通过对对抗性变异字节码进行模糊测试，评估CPython运行时和反编译器的鲁棒性，产生鲁棒性签名；(4) 测试从普通Python源码能否复现字节码层面的发现。

Result: 在1,034,843个PyPI制品中识别出7,388个含字节码的制品，包含228,578个.pyc文件和28,193个制品本地无源码的.pyc文件。对于现代CPython 3.8-3.14字节码，204,904个范围内文件中至少有一个反编译器能对204,901个输出源码（但仅测发射而非验证功能等价）。工具非稳健：PyPI字节码触发托管代码异常和超时，对抗性变异字节码驱动反编译器发生原生进程崩溃，共17种鲁棒性签名。模糊测试产生1,009个堆栈去重后的运行时发现（以指针解引用为主），其中261组表现出潜在内存破坏特征，至少91.7%的组能越过文档声明的非安全边界执行。所有发现均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: 字节码是PyPI生态中显著可见的工件、实际可分析的攻击面目标，也是与源码行为不一定一致的安全相关解释器输入。安全审查不能仅依赖源码层面，需要将字节码作为一等安全工件纳入分析范畴。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [274] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可扩展、与应用无关的框架，用于从大规模区块链活动中进行持久行为模式发现。通过对超过3000万笔以太坊交易构建行为语句并应用两级嵌入（句子级和序列级），结合可解释的行为画像器，该框架能够识别常规和恶意行为模式（如DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和拉地毯骗局），并支持跨观察窗口的长期行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链DeFi分析方法多为应用特定、难以扩展或难以解释，缺乏能够大规模、通用且可解释地发现行为模式的框架，无法满足区块链取证、行为归因和威胁发现的需求。

Method: 框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 构建融入合约、代币和市场上下文的行为语句；(2) 两级嵌入过程——句子级嵌入捕获单个行为，序列级嵌入捕获用户随时间的连续行为；(3) 可解释行为画像器通过行为动机、常规模式、时间动态、实体暴露和可疑性证据来刻画发现的行为社区。

Result: 在以太坊上使用超过3000万笔交易进行评估，成功发现了常规和恶意行为模式，包括DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和拉地毯骗局。许多模式在独立观察窗口中保持稳定，支持长期行为识别。

Conclusion: 该框架同时具备可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析能力，可支持区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现等实际应用。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [275] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一项针对工具使用智能体的安全评估活动开展测量审计，发现历史评分器存在处理组标签泄漏问题（treatment metadata 直接门控 ATTACK_SUCCESS 类别），通过处理组盲的重建修正了58个历史标签，并揭示了锁定v2普查中攻击成功记录为零的事实。最终贡献了"七链完整性"框架和端点完整性检查器，但明确声明该结果为活动范围限定内的测量审计，而非总体攻击率或模型排名估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估中常将"存储的标签"直接等同于"行为事实"，但标签可能受到处理组分配、评分器设计缺陷等非行为因素污染。作者试图通过审计一个真实保留的活动，检验标签与行为的一致性，并揭示评估基础设施中的系统性偏差。

Method: 追踪10,200条执行记录，追溯到180个模型请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激；对比两个schema处理与缺失的外部载荷语料库；发现历史评分器中处理组元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类；执行处理组盲的重建，配以双审阅者盲审核的96个请求一致性审查；构建七链路完整性链与可执行的、范围受限的端点完整性linter。

Result: 处理组盲重建纠正了58个历史ATTACK_SUCCESS或HIJACK_ATTEMPT标签，将其改为合法良性完成，同时保留3个已验证的受保护数据传输和1个独立的未经授权转发案例。锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，转发案例处于语义边界（涉及目标完成）仍为HIJACK_ATTEMPT。双审阅者一致同意96个请求的分类，但与锁定codebook在4个构造边界案例上存在分歧。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个方法论模板：通过可复现的测量审计来验证安全评估中标签的可信度。作者明确划定其声明的适用范围——这是活动限定内的测量审计，不用于估计总体攻击率、模型排名、防御有效性或因果效应。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [276] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）在垃圾短信检测中的对抗鲁棒性，发现经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下几乎灾难性失效（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer模型显著更具韧性（RDR最高0.351），并证明干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的垃圾短信检测系统通常在干净的单语言文本上训练和评估，但在低资源环境中，攻击者常通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动来规避这些系统。这暴露了现有检测方法在对抗设置下的脆弱性，亟需系统评估不同模型架构的对抗鲁棒性差异。

Method: 使用27,037条消息的数据集，评估三种经典词法模型（Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM）和两种多语言Transformer（mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa）。经典模型采用黑盒通用攻击，Transformer采用注意力引导目标攻击。每种模型在三种攻击类型和多种强度级别下进行测试，以鲁棒性退化比（RDR）为度量指标，并辅以Cliff's d效应量分析、Mann-Whitney U检验和Friedman检验进行统计验证。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下RDR高达0.988（近灾难性失效），Transformer模型RDR最高仅0.351。结构扰动是Transformer最显著的弱点。Cliff's d显示两类模型差异显著。Transformer组内，XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更高，但退化程度大于mBERT。统计检验确认这些模式源于模型架构而非采样。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不是对抗鲁棒性的可靠预测指标。研究强调了架构特定防御的必要性，并将垃圾短信检测重新定义为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [277] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HARD框架，一种基于黑客宿主（harness）的自进化运行时防御框架，将LLM代理的运行时防御从手工工程转变为自主进化过程，自动识别干预策略并基于失败轨迹迭代改进防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理日益增强的操作能力引入了复杂的安全威胁。虽然运行时防御已被证明是有效的缓解手段，但现有方法过度依赖手工设计的干预措施，缺乏构建和维护防御机制的原则性框架。

Method: 首先从harness层面建立运行时防御的形式化框架，系统性地刻画harness机制如何支持防御构建，并从harness视角统一已有运行时防御干预措施。在此基础上提出HARD框架，通过自动识别适当的干预策略，并基于观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，实现运行时防御的自主演化。

Result: 大量实验表明，HARD相比现有手工设计的防御方法在安全性能上有提升，同时保持了良性任务效用不被损害。

Conclusion: 研究发现自主防御演化是保护已部署LLM代理的一个有前景的新范式，使代理能够识别自身防御弱点并持续改进保护机制。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [278] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个可观测自主渗透测试代理验证过程的评估框架。通过注入注册的响应变换并将受干扰的episode与原生episode对齐配对，实现对后续动作、证据恢复、停止决策和报告支持的全链路追踪。在450个episode上评估5种模型路线，发现活动量增加可能掩盖验证链断裂，而成功恢复依赖于找到可用证据并在报告中保留它。该框架将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的证据处理探针，将渗透测试代理评估从仅看最终结果扩展到过程级视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试代理依赖目标响应来指导后续动作和最终报告，但欺骗性响应会同时重定向攻击轨迹和验证过程。现有评估仅从最终报告无法了解代理如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或如何将观测转化为漏洞声明，因此需要一种使验证过程可观测的评估框架。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，将每个受变换的episode与相同环境下的原生episode配对，在首个受影响响应处对齐。采用源链接重建方法追踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止行为和报告支持。定义了三个冻结的观测契约：漏洞利用证明、资源所有权和可复用工件。在450个episode上评估五种模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，增加活动量可能掩盖验证链的断裂，而成功的恢复取决于找到可用证据并将其贯穿报告保留下来。五个模型路线在450个episode上的评估揭示了不同模型在证据处理和验证链完整性上的差异。

Conclusion: ATOBench将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的证据处理探针，提供了超越最终结果的渗透测试代理评估的过程级视角，揭示了不受信任的观测如何塑造动作、验证和报告。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [279] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniSphinx，一种新的混合网络数据包格式，允许发送者在数据包中嵌入处理代码，使一个主动混合网络部署能够仿真任何其他混合网络格式（如Sphinx），消除了不同格式间的不兼容性问题。实验表明仿真开销合理：计算时间增加约90μs，头部大小增加33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络存在多种数据包格式，每种格式针对特定目标设计，彼此不兼容，需要部署独立的软件和基础设施，造成资源浪费和维护困难。

Method: 提出OmniSphinx格式，发送者在数据包中嵌入代码来控制数据包的处理方式，构建主动混合网络，使系统能够根据嵌入代码仿真不同的混合网络格式。

Result: 经验评估表明，OmniSphinx在典型混合网络用例中的仿真开销合理：相比Sphinx格式的原生执行，计算时间增加约90μs，头部大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx通过代码嵌入机制实现了在单一部署中统一各种混合网络格式的能力，证明了以合理开销换取格式兼容性是一种可行的方案。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [280] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InterSAGE 是一个信任原生的协议套件，为 LLM 驱动的智能体互操作提供四层安全基底（持久身份、发现、信任协商、问责），通过四个核心原语（Agent 身份卡、基于 DID 的能力感知发现、单调能力衰减的信任协商、内核中介的加密审计轨迹）在不替代现有通信协议的前提下补充缺失的安全语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体互操作协议（如 MCP、A2A 等）主要定义消息交换方式，但缺乏身份证明、授权验证、能力声明和委托后问责机制，这成为物联网中 LLM 智能体跨组织协作的安全关键缺口。

Method: InterSAGE 由四层组成：持久身份层（Agent Identity Cards 绑定开发者、代码包、运营者和部署上下文）、发现层（基于 DID 绑定的可验证凭证清单实现能力感知发现）、信任协商层（单调能力衰减结合两级访问控制）、问责层（内核中介的加密审计轨迹，无需共识账本即可将使用、委托和执行痕迹绑定至智能体身份）。该协议设计为与 MCP、A2A、ANP、AG-UI 互补共存。

Result: 作者将 InterSAGE 与 50 余项工作（涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC 扩展、零信任治理、委托和审计架构）进行了系统比较，结论是没有任何既有架构能作为统一的四层信任基底联合强制实现持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商和问责四大目标。

Conclusion: InterSAGE 为安全智能体互操作提供了缺失的统一信任基底，允许通信协议独立演进的同时保持信任语义的显式性、可移植性和可验证性，填补了现有智能体协议栈中安全层的空白。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [281] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor框架，首次系统性地验证开源SLEIGH处理器规范的正确性。通过自动化代理oracle策略，利用规范自身结构枚举可解码指令形式并生成目标初始状态，将处理器规范驱动的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，在多种架构中发现超过38,920处差异、125个独特bug，并提炼出8条改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规范是反汇编器、反编译器和模拟器等关键安全和程序分析工具的基础，但其正确性很少被检验。规范中的错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并助长分析规避技术，而验证处理器规范是一项极具挑战性的任务。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于自动化代理oracle验证策略的测试框架。该框架利用规范本身编码的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成目标初始状态，然后对处理器规范驱动的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，比较指令的解码和模拟执行结果。

Result: 在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V和MSP430等多种开源规范上应用InSPECtor，发现了超过38,920处差异，最终归结为125个独特bug并提供了修复建议，涵盖解码缺陷、语义缺陷和跨厂商不一致性问题。同时提炼出8条具体建议以推动未来改进。

Conclusion: 该项工作强调了处理器规范正确性的重要性，并提供了一个实用工具来显著提升SLEIGH处理器规范的保真度，从而加强下游安全和分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [282] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究对比了微软GraphRAG（知识图谱检索）与Naive RAG（向量相似度检索）在从网络威胁情报报告自动生成检测规则时的效果，发现GraphRAG生成的检测计划更依赖"痛苦金字塔"顶端的持久性线索（行为模式、TTPs），在攻击者轮换所有IOC（IP、域名、文件哈希）后仍保持100%检测率，而Naive RAG仅剩29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全研究人员发布网络攻击报告后，检测工程师需将其转化为可用的检测规则。但大多数自动化方法只提取简单线索（IP、域名、哈希）并转为阻断列表，攻击者可在数小时或数天内更换这些线索，导致检测快速失效。业界用"痛苦金字塔"描述这一概念——需要生成更多依赖金字塔顶端持久性线索的检测计划。

Method: 将同一份威胁报告分别输入微软GraphRAG（知识图谱检索系统）和Naive RAG（标准向量相似度检索系统），两者使用相同的报告输入、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型，仅检索步骤不同。先对一个APT28报告进行详细案例研究（轮换所有IP、域名和文件哈希后测试检测保持率），再跨9份来自4个厂商的真实CTI报告重复对比实验。

Result: APT28案例研究中，GraphRAG生成的计划在轮换所有IOC后仍保持100%检测触发率，而Naive RAG仅保持29%。在9份跨4个厂商的真实CTI报告上重复实验确认了相同模式：GraphRAG计划一致达到金字塔更高、更难规避的层级，即使两个系统总得分接近时也是如此。

Conclusion: 知识图谱感知检索（knowledge-graph-aware retrieval）应被视为自动生成可部署SOC狩猎计划的架构正确基础；同时提示词措辞的重要性几乎与检索后端本身相当。研究支持将GraphRAG作为从CTI报告自动生成持久有效检测规则的正确技术路线。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [283] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子云存储认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将n个文件的签名次数从n次降为1次，使签名阶段时间与批量大小无关，在n=1000时较逐文件双签名基线提速数百倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端的认证需要抵御未来量子计算机的威胁，因此采用经典原语与标准化后量子原语的混合构造。但将其朴素扩展到多文件上传时，每个文件都需要一次格基签名，签名开销随批量大小线性增长，在真实的批量上传场景下成为性能瓶颈，亟需消除这一每文件签名开销。

Method: 提出承诺驱动的混合后量子模型，由三部分组成：AES-256-GCM批量加密、X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装、Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双签名。核心思路是所有密文先经SHA3-256计算出固定大小的批量承诺摘要，再对该摘要执行一次混合双签名，从而将每批次的签名调用次数从n降为1，与批量大小无关；其余加密与哈希操作受对称密码学高吞吐支撑。

Result: 在普通客户端平台上，进行20次重复取平均，签名阶段时间在批量增大时保持近似恒定，而逐文件基线呈线性增长。n=1000时，对100KB、1MB、10MB文件，模型分别将签名阶段时间降低629、606、725倍（对比除签名方式外共享所有原语的逐文件双签名基线）。

Conclusion: 该承诺型混合后量子模型证实了通过批量承诺聚合签名来消除每文件签名开销的可行性，使后量子安全的多文件上传认证在大批量场景下不再受格基签名开销制约，将瓶颈转移至对称加密与哈希等高性能操作。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [284] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出智能合约不变量（invariant）是防御区块链网络攻击最有力的手段，并通过原创实验协议和自动化工具链（INVARIANTEVAL基准+ PONDEREPLAY回放框架）验证了不变量能阻止全部28个真实以太坊攻击，且通过重放108,637条历史交易证明了其正确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域中最具对抗性的环境，网络犯罪分子利用漏洞窃取了数十亿美元。目前没有概念或技术被证明能真正有效解决这一开放性问题，因此需要寻找更强有力的防御方案。

Method: 设计原创实验协议：(1) 研究不变量如何防御过往真实攻击；(2) 测试最先进的自动化工具能否发现这些不变量。构建INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊攻击事件，每个配有阻止攻击的人写不变量），并用PONDEREPLAY回放框架重放交易以验证不变量的正确性和可靠性。

Result: 智能合约不变量成功阻止了INVARIANTEVAL中所有28个网络犯罪攻击，完整通过回放108,637条历史交易进行了验证。大规模实验清楚证明智能合约不变量能有效抵御网络犯罪分子。

Conclusion: 经典的编程不变量概念在区块链安全领域展现出强大的防御能力，能够系统性阻止真实世界中的智能合约攻击，为区块链安全提供了可行且有实证支撑的解决方案方向。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [285] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过强制执行可验证延迟来限制区块创建者对MEV机会的反应能力，在不破坏活性（liveness）的前提下降低MEV风险。研究在拜占庭设置和理性参与者博弈论模型中均取得了积极结果，同时给出了该防线局限性的负面界，并通过历史MEV数据验证了机制的现实可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV是区块链生态中普遍存在的安全与经济问题，区块创建者可以利用交易排序权提取额外价值，损害用户利益和协议公平性。现有防御手段（如公平排序、阈值加密等）往往面临复杂的信任假设或显著的信令开销，需要一种全新的、轻量且可证明有效的防御机制。

Method: 该机制的核心思想是在生成交易时强制执行可验证延迟（Verifiable Delay），使得区块创建者无法在识别到MEV机会后立即做出反应（如插入或重排交易），同时确保这种延迟不会破坏系统的活性（liveness）。作者分别在拜占庭容错模型和理性参与者博弈论模型中对机制进行了形式化分析，并给出了该防御路线的局限性下界，最后结合历史MEV数据进行了实际部署可行性的实验评估。

Result: 在拜占庭设置和理性参与者博弈模型中均获得了积极结果，证明该机制在特定条件下能有效防御MEV；同时给出了负面界限，揭示了该防御路线的固有局限；基于历史MEV数据的实验表明该机制能够实际阻止大多数现有MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 基于可验证延迟的交易生成机制是一种具有现实可行性的MEV防御方案，在理论与实践中均展现出良好效果，但仍存在固有限制，需要与其他防御手段结合以构建更完整的MEV缓解体系。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [286] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个基于层次-对象-行为（HOB）的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度相似度分析，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的重新打包（即应用克隆）不仅威胁移动用户的安全与隐私，还侵犯了原始应用开发者的版权。然而，现有的检测方法主要针对移动平台（如Android），未能捕捉虚拟现实（VR）的本质特征，因此无法有效检测VR市场中被非法用户频繁攻击的克隆VR应用。

Method: VR-Themis采用两阶段检测框架：1）粗粒度阶段：基于可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，确保框架可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；2）细粒度阶段：对第一阶段识别出的可疑应用进行深入分析，使用自定义的层次-对象-行为（HOB）度量计算相似度。

Result: 在收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且无假阳性，证明该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis通过结合VR应用独特特征的两阶段检测方案，有效解决了VR应用克隆检测问题，在保证检测准确性的同时具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [287] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私状况。通过对278个真实Mini Apps的分析，发现59.4%的应用会联系未披露的第三方，78.8%仅依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或退出机制，暴露了该生态系统中严重的透明度和合规性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为嵌入Telegram客户端的Web应用，用户规模庞大且被广泛使用，但其隐私属性几乎未被探索。与微信等受控执行框架不同，Telegram Mini Apps在WebView中运行，结合平台上下文与标准Web能力，并允许不受限制的出站网络连接，使得敏感信息可能通过普通Web请求被传输给第三方。此外，开发者可以依赖平台提供的默认隐私政策，可能导致泛化的声明无法真实反映单个应用的实际数据实践。

Method: 本文提出了TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架。该框架通过捕获运行时网络流量、识别第三方通信，并将观察到的数据流与公开的隐私信息进行对比，来评估Mini Apps的隐私姿态。研究从tApps Center社区目录中收集了278个可正常工作的Mini Apps作为评估样本。

Result: 实验发现：59.4%的Mini Apps至少联系了一个未披露的第三方；78.8%仅依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策；没有任何Mini App提供同意或退出机制。这些发现揭示了一个广泛使用但研究不足的生态系统中存在的显著透明度与合规性差距。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态存在严重的隐私透明度和合规性问题：大多数应用在未披露的情况下与第三方共享数据，绝大多数依赖默认隐私政策掩盖真实数据实践，且缺乏用户控制机制。该研究强调了这一被忽视生态系统中隐私监管和合规改进的迫切性。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [From Observation to Intervention: Memory in Brains and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12377)
*Morteza Salehjahromi,Shayan A. Zadegan,Amgad Muneer,Jia Wu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过共享的功能性问题（表征位置、线索恢复、信息写入/更新、扰动影响）来比较大脑与大语言模型（LLM）的记忆系统，强调两者的比较是功能性而非解剖性的。人类研究有稀疏概念响应等发现但干预受限，啮齿类研究可有选择性的因果干预，而LLM在记忆本身并不领先，但在实验可及性上具有独特优势。作者主张将LLM作为"实验工具"来生成关于记忆检索、更新、持久性等更清晰的生物学假说，其核心价值在于转移实验逻辑而非解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑和LLM是两种根本不同的记忆系统，但通过共同的功能力学问题可以架起桥梁。作者认识到生物系统与人工系统各有优势与局限：生物系统缺乏选择性干预手段，而LLM虽然缺乏"生活化"情节记忆，却允许对内部状态和存储信息进行直接、可重复的操作。这种不对称性为跨领域研究创造了新的机会。

Method: 本文采用功能性比较框架，从四个共享维度对生物记忆系统与LLM记忆系统进行对照分析：表征位置（突触/神经元集合 vs. 权重/激活）、线索恢复机制（海马-皮层交互 vs. 上下文窗口/检索系统）、信息写入与更新（可塑性 vs. 权重更新）、以及记忆状态扰动（选择性干预 vs. 直接操作）。文章综合了人类、啮齿类和灵长类研究及LLM实证研究的证据，论证各自的实验可及性差异。

Result: 研究发现：人类研究能揭示稀疏概念响应、时间绑定、快速联想形成、情节特异性编码和回忆相关再激活等特征，但选择性干预受限；啮齿类研究提供对学习相关神经元集合的选择性因果访问；人类和猕猴的干预通常影响更广泛的回路；而LLM在缺乏真实情节记忆的同时，却允许对内部状态和存储信息进行异常直接且可重复的操纵。

Conclusion: 作者认为这种不对称性构成新机遇：LLM在记忆本身并不领先，但在实验可及性上领先。LLM的工具可以帮助将关于检索、更新、持久性、可逆性和非预期效应的宽泛问题转化为更锐利的生物学假说。有效的跨领域桥梁在于转移实验逻辑，而非解剖对应关系。

Abstract: Brains and large language models (LLMs) are fundamentally different memory systems, but they can be compared through shared functional questions: where memory-related information is represented, how partial cues recover broader associations, how new information is written or updated, and how memory-related states can be perturbed. In biological systems, these questions span synapses, neuronal ensembles, hippocampal-cortical interactions, and plasticity; in LLMs, they span weights, activations, context windows, retrieval systems, and external stores. The comparison is therefore functional and experimental rather than anatomical. Human studies reveal sparse concept responses, temporal binding, rapid association formation, episode-specific coding, and recall-related reactivation, but selective intervention remains limited. Rodent studies provide more selective causal access to learning-related ensembles, whereas human and macaque interventions usually affect broader circuits. LLMs lack lived episodic memory, yet they permit unusually direct and repeatable manipulation of internal states and stored information. We argue that this asymmetry creates a new opportunity. LLMs are not ahead in memory itself, but in experimental access. Their tools may help turn broad questions about retrieval, updating, persistence, reversibility, and unintended effects into sharper biological hypotheses. The productive bridge is to transfer experimental logic, not anatomical parts.

</details>


### [289] [A Bayes-Markov Neuromorphic Model of Cortical Orientation Selectivity: A Computational Re-implementation and Quantitative Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12388)
*Abolfazl Moslemi,Milad Sarabadani,Fatemeh Sefidian,Hossein Peyvandi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文对Shirazi的Bayes-Markov模型进行了计算层面的显式重实现与定量仿真研究，验证了该框架能从非朝向选择的LGN输入中通过局部推断产生尖锐的朝向选择性，并补充了脉冲神经网络（LIF和HH神经元）的生物学可实现性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V1中朝向选择性的涌现仍是计算神经科学的核心问题。Shirazi的Bayes-Markov模型提供了一个概率解释，但该框架缺乏计算显式的重实现和系统的定量仿真验证，且原始的速率编码SCI场是否能在时间上显式的神经活动中表达尚未得到检验。

Method: 论文重建了Bayes-Markov数学模型，描述了完全由LGN驱动的更新规则，实现了一个向量化的仿真框架以保留原始局部团操作并支持系统参数扫描。评估手段包括朝向调谐曲线、朝向选择性指数（OSI）、受控LGN噪声扰动、对比度测试和模型变体比较。此外，使用漏电积分发放（LIF）和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元实现了SCI层的脉冲化表达，检验速率编码SCI场的时间显式神经活动表达。

Result: 仿真结果支持该框架的核心定性行为：能够产生尖锐的朝向选择性，对中等程度的LGN噪声具有鲁棒性，并提供了生物学上可解释的脉冲化SCI层概念验证，表明推断出的抑制场可以通过时间显式的神经活动来表达。

Conclusion: Bayes-Markov框架的朝向选择性机制在计算显式重实现下得到验证，且其抑制场的速率编码可通过脉冲神经元的时空活动实现，为该理论模型向实验神经科学可检验的方向推进提供了支持。

Abstract: The emergence of orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex (V1) remains a central question in computational neuroscience. Shirazi's Bayes-Markov model proposed a probabilistic explanation for how orientation-selective inhibition can arise from non-oriented lateral geniculate nucleus (LGN) inputs through local inference. In that formulation, the activity pattern of striate cortical inhibitory (SCI) cells is estimated from the LGN activity pattern by a maximum a posteriori (MAP) criterion over a two-layer hierarchical Markov random field, and the resulting inference is implemented through a local parallel relaxation algorithm. We provide a computationally explicit re-implementation and quantitative simulation study of this framework. We reconstruct the mathematical model, describe its fully LGN-driven update rule, and implement a vectorized simulation framework that preserves the original local clique operations while making systematic parameter sweeps feasible. We evaluate the model using orientation tuning curves, an orientation selectivity index (OSI), controlled LGN noise perturbations, contrast tests, and model-variant comparisons. We further add a spiking SCI-layer realization using leaky integrate-and-fire and Hodgkin-Huxley neurons to examine whether the rate-coded SCI field can be expressed through temporally explicit neural activity. The simulations support the central qualitative behavior of the Bayes-Markov framework: sharp orientation selectivity, robustness to moderate LGN noise, and a biologically interpretable proof-of-concept spiking realization of the inferred inhibitory field.

</details>


### [290] [Evaluation Resolution Confounds Learning-Rule Comparisons in Model-Brain RSA of Early Visual Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12408)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文发现，在表征相似性分析（RSA）中，使用小分辨率图像训练的小型卷积网络与大脑视觉皮层进行比较的结果严重依赖于评估分辨率。该研究系统性地检验了多种分辨率（32px至224px）下的V1表征差距，发现生物合理学习规则（如无训练或局部训练）在低分辨率下看起来能与反向传播竞争甚至超越，但在高分辨率下这一结论被推翻——未经训练的网络的V1表征相似性显著低于反向传播训练的网络。研究排除了四种潜在机制，最终定位到图像细节（而非池化）是这一分辨率依赖性的主因，并提出亮度标量可作为该比较范式分辨能力的界限基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSA被广泛用于评估哪种学习规则能让卷积网络产生类脑表征。然而，生物合理的规则（如反馈对齐、预测编码、STDP）难以扩展到大网络，因此研究通常用小网络在32x32 CIFAR图像上训练，再与高分辨率的脑响应模型比较。这种训练与评估分辨率不匹配可能导致误导性结论，本文旨在系统检验这一分辨率依赖性问题。

Method: 作者在六种分辨率（32px到224px）下系统比较未训练/局部训练网络与反向传播训练网络在V1的表征相似性差距，实验涵盖人类fMRI、猕猴电生理、训练轨迹、ImageNet ResNet-50和Swin-Tiny transformer等设置。通过五个机制实验来排除或定位分辨率依赖性的来源，包括训练/评估分辨率匹配、低层Gabor与像素结构、未训练基线的归一化状态、池化描述子收敛到全局亮度统计，以及限制图像细节但扩大池化位置的对照实验。

Result: V1差距从32px分辨率的-0.001±0.007扩大到224px的+0.044±0.006，随分辨率单调增大（n=5种子）。该效应在人类fMRI和单种子猕猴电生理中均得到验证。四种候选机制均无法解释该效应，其中三种通过保持卷积权重完全一致的中断实验被排除。第五个实验（限制图像细节但池化位置扩大12倍）移除了约90%的效应，表明效应主要由图像细节而非池化驱动。亮度标量基准（rho=0.075）与未训练网络的V1 RDM相似性（0.076）几乎一致，为比较范式设定了分辨率边界。唯一跨分辨率稳健的学习效应是LOC区反向传播超过未训练网络。

Conclusion: 低分辨率训练网络与高分辨率脑响应比较会产生误导性结论：未经训练的网络在低分辨率下看似可与反向传播竞争，但在匹配真实图像分辨率的评估下反向传播在V1的优势显现。研究建议此类RSA比较应在足够高（接近自然视觉）的分辨率下进行，并指出亮度标量可作为评估比较范式的下限基准。

Abstract: Representational similarity analysis (RSA) is increasingly used to ask which learning rules give convolutional networks brain-like representations. Because biologically plausible rules such as feedback alignment, predictive coding and STDP do not scale, studies that include them train small networks on small images (typically 32x32 CIFAR) and then compare them to brain responses modeled at much higher resolution. We find that a common result in this setting, that untrained or locally trained networks rival or beat backpropagation at early visual cortex, depends strongly on the resolution at which the network is evaluated. The V1 gap between an untrained network and a backpropagation-trained one widens from -0.001 +/- 0.007 at the 32px training resolution to +0.044 +/- 0.006 at 224px, growing monotonically across six resolutions (n=5 seeds). It holds in human fMRI and, directionally, in single-seed macaque electrophysiology, along the training trajectory, and for an ImageNet ResNet-50 and a Swin-Tiny transformer trained at 224px. Four candidate mechanisms are tested and none accounts for it: train/eval resolution matching, low-level Gabor and pixel structure, the normalization state of the untrained baseline, and convergence of the pooled descriptor toward a global brightness statistic; three are excluded by interventions holding the convolutional weights bit-identical. A fifth experiment locates the effect: capping image detail at the training resolution while letting the pooled positions grow 12-fold removes about 90% of it, so the dependence is carried by image detail rather than by pooling. Separately, a single scalar luminance value per image reaches rho = 0.075 against the V1 RDM, essentially matching the untrained network's 0.076, which bounds what this style of comparison can resolve. The one learning effect that holds across resolution is backprop above untrained, at LOC.

</details>


### [291] [A Hierarchical Energy-Based Model for Multimodal Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12398)
*Subir Varma*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: IM-LEPP（集成多模态潜能量基预测处理）是一种分层、基于能量的多模态认知模型，将先前单模态LEPP模型扩展以整合视觉与语言。该模型以枢纽-分支层级架构为基础，将视觉对象、场景和语言单元的预测编码管道汇聚到一个模拟前颞叶的共享无模态枢纽上，认知被建模为潜状态在学习的能量景观中流动而非对神经环路的直接描述。该架构为注意力现象（如非注意盲视、Necker立方体双稳态）提供了机制解释，并恢复或激发了心理语言学的既定发现（惊奇理论、N400/P600 ERP成分、花园路径重分析），同时提出与Transformer语言模型在轨迹敏感性上的可证伪对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前LEPP模型仅处理单一模态，缺乏整合视觉与语言的多模态认知框架。作者旨在构建一个统一的多模态认知计算模型，既能提供机制性解释（而非仅描述性），又能弥合认知科学、心理语言学与计算机视觉/自然语言处理之间的鸿沟，同时保持与生成式神经网络作为认知动力学的有效理论这一观念一致。

Method: 采用枢纽-分支（hub-and-spoke）层级架构，基于Lambon Ralph等人的受控语义认知框架：视觉对象、场景和语言单元的预测编码管道（spokes）汇聚到一个模拟前颞叶的共享无模态枢纽（hub）。每个管道自身的预测被当前枢纽状态条件化而非覆盖，从而保留管道特异性身份同时反映完整的多模态上下文。认知被建模为潜状态在能量景观中的流动，使用能量基模型（能量景观学习）作为核心机制，类比统计力学与热力学的关系。

Result: 架构给出了注意力现象（非注意盲视、Necker立方体双稳态）的机制性解释；结构恢复或激发了心理语言学的既定发现，包括惊奇理论、N400/P600 ERP成分和花园路径重分析；提出了与Transformer语言模型在轨迹敏感性（下一词预测中的路径依赖）方面的可证伪对比；讨论了相对于LLM的数据高效语言习得；概述了语义/情景记忆子系统。

Conclusion: IM-LEPP为多模态认知提供了一个统一的分层能量基框架，在机制解释力上与现有发现对接，同时提出可证伪的实验预测。该模型定位于预测加工、自由能原理、JEPA和层级时间记忆等理论谱系中，可作为理解多模态认知动力学的有效理论，但需通过具体实验预测来验证其核心主张。

Abstract: We propose IM-LEPP (Integrated Multimodal Latent Energy-based Predictive Processing), a hierarchical, energy-based model of multimodal cognition that extends a previously proposed single-modality model (LEPP) to integrate vision and language. Following the view that generative neural networks are effective theories of cognitive dynamics, analogous to how statistical mechanics relates to thermodynamics, IM-LEPP models cognition as latent states flowing through learned energy landscapes rather than as an account of neural circuitry. The architecture is a hub-and-spoke hierarchy, grounded in the controlled semantic cognition framework of Lambon Ralph et al., in which predictive-coding pipelines for visual objects, scenes, and linguistic units converge on a shared amodal hub modeled on the anterior temporal lobe. Each pipeline's own prediction is conditioned by, rather than overwritten by, the current hub state, preserving pipeline-specific identity while letting every prediction reflect the full multimodal context. We show this architecture gives a mechanistic account of attentional phenomena such as inattentional blindness and Necker-cube bistability, and that its structure recovers or motivates independently established findings in psycholinguistics, including surprisal theory, the N400/P600 ERP components, and garden-path reanalysis, alongside a falsifiable contrast with transformer language models on trajectory-sensitivity in next-word prediction. We also discuss data-efficient language acquisition relative to LLMs, outline a semantic/episodic memory subsystem, situate the model against predictive coding, the free-energy principle, JEPA, and Hierarchical Temporal Memory, and propose concrete experimental predictions to test its central claims Key Words: predictive processing; predictive coding; energy-based models; diffusion models; effective theory; computational neuroscience.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统一基准竞赛，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界评测相结合，旨在评估和提升机器人操作策略的泛化能力。比赛提供基于Transformer、扩散模型、VLA和World-Action-Model的基线实现，最终仅在未见过的真实操作环境中进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中的通用机器人操作仍然是一个核心挑战，尽管模型架构和学习算法进展迅速，但真实世界数据的稀缺和多样性不足严重限制了进展。现有基准缺乏跨任务、环境和难度级别的统一评估框架。

Method: RoboSynChallenge提出了一个统一基准，整合大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评测。参赛者被鼓励利用合成状态-动作轨迹改进通用策略学习，同时提供多种基线实现（Transformer、Diffusion、VLA和World-Action-Model）以确保可复现性和可比性。最终评估仅在未见过的真实操作环境中进行。

Result: 通过将可扩展的仿真训练与严格的真实世界验证相结合，RoboSynChallenge旨在促进开发广泛能力、数据高效且适应性强的操作系统，为通用机器人智能铺平道路。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过耦合仿真训练与真实世界验证，为衡量操作策略的泛化能力提供了一个标准化平台，有望推动数据高效且适应性强的通用机器人操作系统的发展。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [293] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attune的预部署启发式工具，利用操作员眼部注视行为作为机器人行为设计的线索，帮助设计者理解并校准多机器人监控界面中的操作员注意力分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人监督场景中，管理操作员的注意力是界面设计的核心挑战，涵盖布局和机器人行为设计两方面。目前设计者缺乏关于如何通过改变机器人行为来捕捉、维持或放弃操作员注意力的实证指导，需要一种工具来弥合这一空白。

Method: Attune工具自动识别有意义的注视转移事件，提供AI辅助来标注注视转移的原因，并输出操作员注视模式的摘要供其审阅。通过用户研究让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素来评估工具效果。

Result: 研究结果揭示了观察到的注视模式存在差异，并展示了Attune如何帮助表征操作员注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune作为预部署启发式工具能够有效支持设计者理解操作员注意力模式，为未来根据操作员注意力特征调整机器人行为提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [294] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性定位（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）任务，并设计FUSE框架，通过结合显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习得到的摊余规划器，使具身智能体能够主动选择信息丰富的视角，在遮挡或不完整线索下按功能而非身份定位物体。实验表明FUSE在非oracle定位性能上达到最高，计算量较完全显式探索降低1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需基于功能而非物体身份来识别和交互目标，但现有可供性定位方法仅从固定视角出发，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时主动决策"应该往哪里看"的机制。

Method: 提出FUSE（自适应语义-几何证据采集框架），包含两个核心组件：1) 显式不确定性驱动的探索机制，用于识别信息不足的区域；2) 学习得到的摊余规划器（amortized planner），用于高效选择信息丰富的视角。同时构建了基于Habitat仿真平台的基准测试环境。

Result: FUSE在非oracle定位性能上达到最优，相比完全显式的探索方法计算开销降低1.33倍，且在多种可供性知识源下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: 将主动感知与可供性定位结合是可行且高效的路线；通过学习摊余规划器在保留探索质量的同时显著降低计算成本，为具身智能体的功能性物体定位提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [295] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个全在线、零样本的主动感知框架，通过增量构建可查询的空间语义表示并进行主动视点验证，解决了层次化开放词汇目标导航（OVON）在部分观测环境下的空间定位与目标验证难题，在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化OVON要求智能体根据涵盖场景、房间、区域和实例层级线索的自由形式指令在未见环境中导航。已有工作LangMap虽形式化了该设定，但在部分观测下可靠解决仍具挑战：空间定位需要持久的全局环境证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有区分度的候选视图，两者难以兼顾。

Method: SAP-Nav包含两个核心组件：(1) 从主动获取的房间视图增量构建可查询的空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），支持从任意已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视点验证（Active Viewpoint Verification），评估当前观测是否提供足够证据，必要时将智能体重新定位到更具信息量的视点，再依据类别和属性约束验证候选目标。无需任务特定训练或预计算场景地图。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上，SAP-Nav在所有层次上取得整体最佳性能，其中区域级导航的SR相比基于训练的方法提升了12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了其实用可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav通过主动感知策略同时满足空间定位的证据积累与目标验证的视点质量要求，在零样本条件下超越了训练方法，证明了主动视觉信息获取对于解决层次化OVON的重要性，且具备真实世界的落地能力。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [296] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统（MRS）分散式协作搬运方法，通过地形可穿越性分析将非结构化环境转化为二维可穿越地图，利用遗传算法优化模糊推理系统（FIS）以生成机器人速度，在避开障碍物的同时最小化总路径长度，并在多种测试场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在非结构化环境中执行协作物体搬运任务时，需要同时解决路径规划和障碍物规避问题。现有方法在复杂地形下往往面临全局路径长度优化不足、计算复杂度高以及对环境适应性差等挑战，因此需要一种能够在离线训练中学习、在线分散执行的高效决策机制。

Method: 该方法包含两个关键环节：（1）基于高程图的地形可穿越性分析，利用坡度信息降低维度并将不可穿越区域识别为障碍物，将地图转换为二维可穿越地图；（2）采用遗传算法优化多个模糊推理系统（FISs），在包含局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物和杂乱环境等多种训练场景下，优化生成多机器人系统的搬运速度。训练完成的FIS模型随后应用于测试环境。

Result: 在多种训练场景下训练的FIS模型被成功应用于转换后的可穿越地图测试环境，并通过多个测试场景进行了验证。结果表明该方法能够有效完成协作搬运任务并最小化MRS总路径长度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明遗传模糊系统能够有效解决多机器人在非结构化环境下的协作搬运问题，通过地形分析降维和离线训练，实现了分散式的路径优化与避障，为复杂环境下多机器人协作任务提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [297] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，通过"当前到未来"的空间地图想象机制解决无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）中的显式场景锚定和未来感知空间推理不足的问题，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法大多直接将视觉-语言输入映射到动作，缺乏显式的场景锚定（explicit scene grounding）和未来感知的空间推理能力，难以应对复杂户外环境下从稀疏多视角观测推断空间结构并执行可行3D运动的挑战。

Method: AirForesight包含三个核心组件：(1) 从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，通过当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测联合监督；(2) 在结构化因果注意力机制下将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；(3) 聚合当前与未来表示以预测下一个3D航点，并引入跨空间规划一致性损失，促使预测的地图空间轨迹与真实航点位移导出的专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S两个数据集上的实验以及大量消融研究表明，该框架性能优异，验证了其有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码当前场景结构和未来运动意图，并利用跨空间一致性约束使空间想象与导航目标对齐，AirForesight为UAV-VLN提供了一种有效的未来感知空间推理框架。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [298] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一种结合自适应节点分配（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）的连续时间视觉惯性导航系统，通过运动自适应样条轨迹建模在不同运动与传感条件下实现了优于基线的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间视觉惯性导航系统（如基于IMU预积分的方法）虽然精度和效率较高，但基于关键帧的表示在残差需在任意时间戳评估或需要运动相关的时间分辨率时不够灵活。连续时间样条方法虽能解决这一问题，但均匀分布的节点在快速动态区域表示不足、在静态区间则过度参数化。因此需要一种能够自适应分配节点、实现多分辨率表示的连续时间VINS框架。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应节点分配（AKP），根据局部运动变化量分配样条节点；(2) 多分辨率样条（MRS），在切空间中添加有界局部细化；(3) 3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR），通过在3D测量空间中对齐变换后的特征与校准观测射线来实现轴承一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明，ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动和传感条件下取得了与基线相当或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: ASPIRE-VINS证明了将自适应节点分配、多分辨率样条表示和3D测量空间残差相结合的连续时间VINS框架，能够灵活应对任意时间戳的残差评估需求并自适应调节时间分辨率，在轨迹精度上具备竞争力，为连续时间视觉惯性导航提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [299] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个用于自动驾驶的统一规划框架，通过结构化动作空间协调将语义推理（VLA）与预测性世界建模（WAM）结合，并在NAVSIM v1和v2上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要同时满足语义约束和预测动力学，但现有端到端方法往往只强调其中一个方面：VLA模型利用VLM先验进行语义推理，WAM通过生成式世界建模提供未来感知预测。然而简单的联合token级注意力组合会导致注意力分配不匹配问题，即语义捷径主导共享注意力空间并抑制预测动力学。

Method: 受神经科学中复杂行为源于功能特化系统间协调的启发，BrainWAM将语义推理和预测世界建模转化为两个专门的行动导向通路，并在紧凑动作表示层面进行对齐。同时引入异步修正流推理策略，采用解耦的视频与动作去噪，在缩短推理延迟的同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均优于VLA-only和WAM-only方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: BrainWAM通过结构化动作空间协调有效解决了VLA与WAM联合时的注意力分配不匹配问题，证明了语义先验与预测动力学可以在同一规划框架中互补共存，为自动驾驶规划系统提供了实用且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [300] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了HumanoidVLN，一个基于物理的仿真器和基准测试，用于跨多种人形机器人平台的视觉语言导航任务，覆盖4种具身形态、4种VLN模型，并展示了仿真到现实的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准测试主要面向轮式智能体，未考虑双足行走的物理约束、人形机器人形态的多样性，以及由步态运动引起的自我中心观测畸变，因此缺乏适用于人形机器人VLN的物理仿真平台和基准。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建HumanoidVLN平台，集成层级控制栈（强化学习步态策略+可互换的PD或MPC路径跟踪器）；支持4种人形机器人（Unitree G1/H1及两种内部机器人）；环境来自艺术家设计场景和3D高斯溅射重建，筛选可导航面积超100平方米；采用双生成器-评审器加改写的多智能体管道生成带人工校验的指令，产生933条碰撞感知参考轨迹。

Result: 在4个模型和4种具身形态上，JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20集仿真到真机试点实验中，导航误差强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对误差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度0.782±0.188 nDTW。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了VLN模型、控制器与人形机器人具身形态在物理执行下的交互作用，验证了HumanoidVLN作为人形机器人VLN研究平台的有效性和仿真到现实的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [301] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于主动LED标记的自主水下机器人（AUV）相对位姿估计框架，通过红色中心LED和三个蓝色外围LED组成的紧凑标记模块，结合概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），在复杂水下环境中实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计，并通过水槽实验和闭环领航-跟随实验验证了其精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探测和采样至关重要，但水下环境存在浊度、光照变化、反射和特征遮挡等严重光学退化，使基于视觉的相对定位面临挑战，亟需一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出AMR-Pose框架：1) 开发由1个红色中心LED和3个蓝色外围LED组成的紧凑标记模块安装于领航AUV上，提供独特视觉特征；2) 设计概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），融合SE(3)上李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计；3) 根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性切换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 水槽实验利用运动捕捉系统作为真值验证了AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现准确、平滑、鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环领航-跟随实验进一步证明了其在协同水下机器人中实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的结合，有效解决了水下光学退化环境下的AUV相对位姿估计难题，为协同AUV系统的实时相对定位提供了一种可靠且实用的方案。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [302] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO方法，解决GRPO-based VLA策略后训练中的"轨迹级信用混淆"问题，通过构建可检测的任务阶段、将轨迹对齐到阶段的动作区间，并仅比较处于相同阶段的轨迹来计算阶段优势，从而提升VLA策略在任务成功率和样本效率上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于GRPO的VLA后训练中，一个全局的轨迹级优势被应用于轨迹中的每个动作，导致一个完成多个有效阶段但最终失败的轨迹会惩罚其早期的正确动作，即轨迹级信用混淆问题。这阻碍了策略的有效学习。

Method: Temporal GRPO包含三个关键步骤：(1) 构建可检测的任务阶段；(2) 将每个轨迹对齐到特定阶段的动作区间；(3) 仅比较进入相同阶段的轨迹来计算阶段级优势。然后将阶段优势应用于对应的动作区间，并在单次策略更新中完成。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和样本效率，且在不同任务长度上均有一致的性能提升。在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验表明，该方法能保留共享的前提阶段，并集中改进轨迹结果开始分化的第一个阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过细粒度的阶段级信用分配有效缓解了轨迹级信用混淆问题，为VLA策略的后训练提供了更精确、更高效的优化路径。
