Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [303] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H2R-Bench，一个用于评估跨具身（cross-embodiment）人-机器人操作视频生成的基准测试框架。该基准将人类第一视角演示视频转换为指定机器人具身下的操作视频，并通过五个维度（目标状态完成、动作事件完成、功能接触转移、具身正确性、视频质量）对十一个最先进的视频生成模型进行系统评估，揭示现有视频世界模型在人体到机器人操作迁移方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作数据对机器人学习至关重要，但机器人演示数据收集成本高且难以规模化。虽然大量人类第一视角操作视频蕴含丰富的行为经验，但由于人手与机器人末端执行器之间的差异，跨具身迁移面临挑战。视频世界模型有望从人类观察中合成以机器人为中心的操作视频，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统探索，因此需要建立专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 作者构建了H2R-Bench基准，包含：1）人类演示视频、目标具身约束以及源接地的标注（覆盖任务目标、动作事件、功能接触和物体响应）；2）从五个维度评估生成视频：目标状态完成、动作事件完成、功能接触转移、具身正确性和通用视频质量；3）对跨六个操作家族、两个机器人具身的十一个最先进视频生成模型进行系统基准测试。

Result: 实验评估显示当前视频世界模型在人体到机器人操作迁移方面能力有限：即使领先模型在具身一致性（embodiment consistency）、功能交互（functional interaction）和任务执行（task execution）上经常失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench提供了一个系统化的诊断框架，用于评估视频世界模型是否能弥合人体与机器人之间的具身差距，并将人类操作观察转化为面向机器人的训练资源。现有模型在该任务上的表现表明这一方向仍需进一步研究与发展。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [304] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的训练与评估模拟器。SRF将预训练视觉模型产生的2D语义分割提升到3D辐射场中，同时编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，为空间推理智能体提供既几何保真又语义可查询的真实世界多样化环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体的空间推理训练需要既几何保真又具备可查询语义的多样化环境。合成模拟器提供真实语义但牺牲真实性；基于真实世界重建的模拟器外观真实但缺乏默认的标注语义。二者之间存在不可调和的矛盾，需要一种统一表示。

Method: 提出语义辐射场（SRF）作为模拟器表示：从真实场景的带姿态RGB采集中将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场中，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份。该表示支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并通过物理引擎驱动的示例应用（果园苹果抓取任务）展示其作为模拟器的能力。

Result: SRF能够在统一的接地表示中同时支持新视角渲染、语义标注和占用查询，实现高效生成多样化的真实世界环境用于空间推理模型的训练与评估。示例展示了SRF驱动的果园苹果抓取模拟器，其中辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占用查询。

Conclusion: 语义辐射场（SRF）弥合了合成模拟器与真实重建模拟器之间的差距，为空间推理智能体提供了兼具几何保真度和语义可查询性的真实世界模拟环境，是训练和评估空间推理模型的一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [305] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出S2-HWM（稀疏事件结构化分层世界模型），通过从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，协调事件级管理器和原始步工作器，实现长视野手术机器人操纵。在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上达到98.7%的成功率，比平坦GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野手术机器人操纵任务奖励稀疏，有意义的交互变化以不规则间隔发生。现有世界模型以原始步分辨率进行想象，无法显式建模可变时长任务进度；手动指定阶段虽然能提供中间结构，但其任务特定边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM由三个关键组件构成：(1) 从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度管理器目标更新；(2) 事件级管理器在事件边界处选择潜目标，条件化原始步工作器的动作直至下一次更新；(3) 事件转换模型（ETM）利用学习到的事件证据形成可变时长分段，预测下一边界随机状态、分段持续时间和累积分段奖励，通过链式事件级预测提供超越原始想象视野的可变时长延续。

Result: 在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM达到98.7%的成功率，相比平坦GAS DreamerV3基线提升22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过自动学习稀疏事件结构，无需手动指定任务阶段边界，有效解决了长视野手术机器人操纵中奖励稀疏和交互间隔不规则的问题，证明了事件结构化分层世界模型在长时程操纵任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [306] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂，通过两个四旋翼推进模块与被动关节及齿轮驱动伺服相结合，实现解耦的6自由度末端执行器控制和高扭矩物理交互能力，并以0.447 kg总质量实现1.019 N·m的最大扭矩（扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中操作器存在强位置-姿态耦合问题，而全驱动平台设计往往面临结构复杂、载荷能力有限和扭矩输出不足等挑战，难以满足空中物理交互任务对大作用力和大扭矩的需求。

Method: FAM-DQ采用双四旋翼设计，将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装于中央框架两端，并利用齿轮驱动的伺服机构调控指向方向，从而实现6自由度末端执行器解耦控制与全向操作能力。

Result: 实验验证了轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和拧螺丝等任务。FAM-DQ实现最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过创新的双四旋翼布局与被动关节/齿轮驱动伺服设计，有效解决了全驱动空中操作器的结构和扭矩输出难题，为高扭矩空中物理交互任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [307] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂软体气动执行器的制造难题，系统评估了热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液体增材制造和FDM等多种制造工艺，发现FDM因可通过工艺优化逐步消除主导缺陷而最具适应性，并提出以制造约束与执行器架构兼容性为导向的面向制造设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器需要同时满足几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，现有工艺路线难以兼顾这些要求，缺乏系统性的工艺评估与选择指导。

Method: 对多种制造路线（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液体基增材制造、FDM）进行工艺筛选、基准制造、失效分析和工艺改进，以区分工艺固有局限与可纠正的制造缺陷，并评估气密性与挤出头路径架构及壁厚的关系。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何贴合度，浇铸受限于模具可达性和粘接界面，粉末基方法受限于封闭通道内残留材料，数字光处理受限于材料特性和后处理要求；FDM的可纠正缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除，且气密性不仅取决于标称壁厚还取决于挤出路径架构，无支撑几何对内部后处理受限场景至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的面向制造设计方法，通过评估执行器架构与制造约束的兼容性来指导工艺选择，为开发复杂、柔顺且气密的软体气动执行器提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [308] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级模块化主动避碰框架，在末端执行器速度指令层面工作。它通过基于碰撞时间（TTC）预测并集成超调保护，结合罗德里格斯旋转公式偏转相对速度向量来实现避障，同时保持速度大小不变，缓解了传统人工势场法的死锁问题。仿真与实物实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作使机械臂能够执行类人灵巧动作，但在网络延迟或操作员注意力受限时，人类操作者可能无法及时有效地回避突然出现的障碍物，造成安全风险。现有的碰撞避免方法（如APF）存在死锁问题，且难以满足遥操作中高频控制的需求。

Method: 该方法是一个运行在末端执行器速度指令层的轻量级模块化框架，包含三个关键环节：（1）点云预处理；（2）基于碰撞时间（TTC）并集成超调保护的碰撞预测模块，用于补偿复杂遥操作流程引入的点云处理延迟；（3）利用罗德里格斯旋转公式对相对速度向量进行偏转，仅改变方向而保持速度大小不变，从而规避APF方法的死锁问题。

Result: 在多种仿真场景中，所提方法相比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免成功率；实物机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其碰撞避免的有效性。轻量化设计使其能够支持遥操作所需的高频控制。

Conclusion: 该主动避碰框架通过速度层面的预测-偏移机制，有效提升了遥操作机械臂在动态环境中的安全性，兼顾了实时性与避碰性能，并解决了传统APF方法固有的死锁问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [309] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一种嵌套式策略学习框架，通过让操作员用单自由度离合器调节预训练的"手部技能"而非直接指定手指轨迹来采集演示，从而简化灵巧操作的演示收集过程，并在真实实验中提升了演示可靠性与自主策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作虽能显著增强机器人对物理世界的交互能力，但学习此类行为受限于难以收集一致且完整的任务演示。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作员在整个任务过程中协调手臂运动与精确的、接触丰富的手指行为，这导致直接演示收集极其困难。

Method: NestDex提出嵌套式策略学习框架：(1) 操作员控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节激活的内层手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹；(2) 内层手部策略依据最新本体感觉历史自适应调整运动，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活适当的技能；(3) 采集到的演示用于训练一个独立的外层视觉运动策略，该策略在部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂与手；(4) 手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保持手臂命令处于关节空间。

Result: 在多种真实世界灵巧操作实验中，NestDex显著提升了演示收集的可靠性与效率，实证评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过嵌套式策略设计，以"学习的手部技能+操作员高层调节"替代繁琐的直接手指标注，有效降低了灵巧操作演示采集的门槛，并验证了该范式下自主策略学习的可行性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [310] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA 是一种面向自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的失败感知自进化框架。当GRPO采样的轨迹质量低且多样性不足时，它利用冻结的轮次起点模型副本进行自蒸馏，将未解决的失败转化为特权监督信号，从而显著降低规划误差和评估持久性失败率（在150个nuScenes场景、6019个样本上，G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500m，失败率从13.03%降至11.20%），同时保持单样本规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GRPO的强化学习方法依赖组内轨迹间的相对奖励差异来更新策略。但当所有采样轨迹都表现不佳时，这种相对信号只能对失败进行排序，无法识别失败区域之外的正确行为，导致策略在失败状态下缺乏有效监督信号，难以自我纠正。

Method: FIRE-VLA提出了失败触发式自蒸馏机制：1）检测低奖励、低多样性组作为失败信号触发器；2）从冻结的轮次起点模型副本中提取特权监督（教师与学生的参数规模相同，但教师能观察到隐藏的未来轨迹）；3）监督仅作用于答案token并跟随学生生成的前缀；4）GRPO在所有组上保持激活，两种学习信号并行；5）更新后的策略作为下一轮的教师，使路由的失败分布随策略演化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT检查点上，匹配学生rollout与策略更新次数后，FIRE-VLA在6,019个来自150个nuScenes场景的测试样本上：保持可比较的单样本规划性能，将G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500m（提升约18.8%），评估持久性失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。误差降低主要来源于减少罕见严重rollout，而非对普通轨迹的均匀改善。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过将GRPO无法解析的失败转化为特权自我监督，实现了无需更大外部教师的自进化闭环。其性能提升主要来自对极端失败轨迹的纠正，表明针对失败分布的定向监督比均匀优化更有效。该框架为自动驾驶VLA模型提供了自适应的失败感知训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [311] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）的集成方法，通过在电机安装支架引入柔性元件实现微小变形下的实时缆绳张力测量，并结合基于多体短粗梁模型的并行计算框架和近端多轴力/力矩传感器，实现单段和双段构型的绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人的实时形状感知与接触力/位置感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人的形状与力感知长期面临关键瓶颈，主要难点在于在受限的绞盘组件空间内难以获取缆绳张力信息。传统方案需要在紧凑空间中额外布置传感器，与小型化需求冲突。

Method: 1）引入符合"驱动-感知协同设计"理念的柔性元件，安装于电机安装支架，在缆绳反作用力下产生微小变形，实现不占用紧凑绞盘空间的实时缆绳张力测量；2）针对空间缆绳布线策略带来的非常规关节构型，提出基于多体短粗梁模型的并行计算框架，可捕获短梁段剪切效应及多缆绳协同交互，同时满足实时性要求；3）引入近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，实现稳定的形状与力感知。

Result: 在单段和双段构型的绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人上进行了实验验证，实现了实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力和接触位置感知，验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现缆绳张力反馈，使集成感知能力在绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人中成为可行。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [312] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动技能学习算法Deliberate Practice (DP)，在有限的练习预算下，通过双线性规划精确计算预算最优的技能练习分配方案，使机器人最大化长期任务中的累积奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在顺序任务中自主习得技能时面临有限的练习预算，如何在预算约束下选择练习哪些技能以最大化长期收益是一个关键问题。由于需要推理组合规模的技能计划和庞大的预算，计算预算最优分配极具挑战性。

Method: 提出DP算法，估计掌握技能所需时间和技能解锁任务计划的累积奖励；将该优化问题建模为双线性规划，利用现成求解器精确求解预算最优的技能练习分配。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时域操作任务实验中，该方法使机器人能够在有限的练习时间内最优地获取有用策略并改善长期规划性能。

Conclusion: DP算法能够在有限练习预算下实现可证明的预算最优技能分配，有效提升机器人长时域操作任务的学习效率与规划能力。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [313] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Seeker，一种从动作监督中学习视觉注意力的任务与状态条件化读出机制，基于冻结的DINOv3特征迭代更新查询向量，生成随任务进展变化的兴趣区域（ROI），用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤，在仿真和真实机器人上显著提升数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉瓶颈方法依赖外部空间标签（如注视点、物体类别、可供性标注）来定义兴趣区域。虽有无标签替代方案通过端执行器投影位置生成裁剪，但其编码了固定的事件时机、代理点和裁剪尺度，当视觉证据远离末端执行器或随任务进度连续变化时会产生错位，限制了数据高效的视动学习。

Method: Seeker是一种任务条件和状态条件的读出机制，从动作中学习注意力。它从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代地以收集的视觉证据更新一个查询向量，仅依靠动作监督产生进度感知的ROI。学到的ROI作为空间接口，用于三种用途：RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中，Seeker相比无裁剪、增强和动作衍生裁剪基线，在数据效率和鲁棒性上均有提升。真实机器人实验中，Seeker将域内平均成功率从最优基线的48.3%提升到76.7%，在光照和背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升到60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker证明了从动作监督中学习进度感知的注意力区域是可行的，能够替代依赖外部标签或固定启发式规则的ROI接口，有效解决视觉证据与末端执行器不一致时的错位问题，同时显著提升真实场景中的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [314] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactGuard是一个面向分块视觉运动策略的接触前执行监控器。它利用潜在世界模型在策略执行动作块前预测短时程后果，并通过轻量级故障探针判断预测的潜在表示是否预示失败，从而在机器人实际接触物体之前中止有问题的动作。该方法无需像素级视频预测，仅需未标注的机器人轨迹训练潜在世界模型及少量标注的接触前片段训练故障探针，在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，失败通常只能在机器人真正接触物体之后才被检测到，尤其在手部相机（wrist-camera）设置下更为严重——近距离的抓取器-物体视图虽然有助于观察接触，但糟糕的接近动作可能在传统检测器反应之前就已经造成推挤、错过、滑动或扰动物体。因此需要在接触发生之前就预测并中止可能的失败。

Method: ContactGuard包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在世界模型——从未标注的机器人轨迹中训练，在规划动作下预测紧凑的多视角视觉嵌入（而非像素级视频预测）；(2) 轻量级故障探针——从少量标注的接触前片段中训练，判断预测的潜在表示是否指示失败。在部署时，ContactGuard在即将发生的接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下前向滚动模型，并验证预测的接触后潜在表示。该方法不修改底层策略。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard在预测失败方面优于直接消融和损坏动作消融基线，并能作为接触前中止信号实时迁移到真机机器人上，且无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard证明通过潜在空间的世界模型预测，可以在机器人实际接触物体之前识别即将失败的动作块，从而为分块视觉运动策略提供有效的接触前失败检测与中止机制，显著降低了接触丰富操作的失败风险。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [315] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，解决社交机器人在域增量持续学习场景下的问题，即机器人需在逐域接触不同环境（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）时，同时利用环境和社交线索学习适当的行动，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需在不同环境中运行，相似布局可能对应不同社交适当行为（如拥挤家庭中可开始对话，但办公室会议中则不妥）。由于规范和场景无法预先全部知晓，机器人需要持续学习以从序列经验中适应并保留旧知识。已有工作研究了生成社交适当行为的持续学习，但未覆盖域增量设定，即机器人逐域接触多样环境，且环境线索和社交线索共同决定行动的适当性。

Method: 提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架：显式地将环境相关知识和社交代理相关知识分离为两个分支，并采用基于回放（replay-based rehearsal）的机制来缓解遗忘，同时学习在多个室内域中的机器人行为适当性（如清洁、服务、开始对话）。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个室内域上优于若干最先进的基线方法；消融研究进一步评估了不同的解耦策略以及模型对域顺序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD框架通过显式解耦环境和社交知识并配合回放机制，有效解决了社交机器人域增量持续学习中环境与社交线索联合作用下的行为适当性学习问题，且代码已公开。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [316] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法，在视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）π₀.₅的残差流中发现"任务进度"（归一化剩余时间）是可线性读取的内部表征信号，该信号在预训练主干中已存在，可用于无标签的分布外检测以监测部署中的VLA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA正快速走向实际部署，但我们缺乏理解这些模型内部表征以及运行时监控它们的基本工具。

Method: 借鉴机制可解释性思想，对VLA模型π₀.₅的残差流进行线性探针分析，检测任务进度信号的可读性，并利用该探针构建无标签OOD检测器。

Result: 任务进度信号在线性层面上可从激活中读出，且存在于预训练PaliGemma主干中；线性探针可泛化到未见任务，在多提示数据训练下随语言反事实变化，但无法有效引导策略；作为OOD检测器在检测停滞任务进度方面与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部具有丰富的、线性可读的语义量（如任务进度）的表征，学习读取这些信号为监测部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [317] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个浏览器原生的海洋滑翔机规划器数字测试场地，将所选区域转化为可复现的四维实验环境，通过统一的"规划-观测"契约连接车辆、传感与评估假设，实现无需安装、无需稀缺资源的滑翔机规划器在位置复评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复在位置评估极其困难，需要稀缺的载具、操作人员、布放与回收资源，且海洋条件无法为竞争算法重置，导致算法之间难以进行公平、可复现的比较。

Method: 构建一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字测试场地，流程涵盖区域选择、任务范围水深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标、可选任务分解、航路规范、流场平流执行、观测生成与评分。核心是一个统一的规划到观测契约，统一了手动航路、内置算法与导入的经典/学习型规划器的假设；通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的源锁定原生到浏览器一致性。采用受控OSSE实验评估五个经典规划器，包含两个场景、三个确定性种子和60小时校准水平线。

Result: 54个任务全部完成并回收且无硬性违规；规划器排名与潜水策略效应揭示了运行-科学权衡。一次真实的公开部署提供了现场参考审计以界定当前运动学边界。GliderFlight 1.2.0实现了原生到浏览器的一致性。

Conclusion: 该运行空间在科学上是可追溯的，且组件经过资格验证，可用于任务规模的部署前实验。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [318] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准和HumanScore评估指标，解决人形运动追踪评估与人类感知不一致的问题。基准包含约153小时光动捕轨迹和文本标签，HumanScore基于12K运动对训练，能更好地预测人类偏好并发现动力学错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动追踪评估依赖运动学误差（如逐帧位姿差异），与人类在视频中的感知不一致，忽视了不稳定支撑和错误接触（如脚滑、触地时机不当）等物理伪影；同时现有测试集规模小且缺乏多样性，无法充分评估接触密集、长时程的行为。

Method: 构建HumanTracker基准，包含约153小时来自多位专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四种运动族并附带文本标签以实现细粒度诊断；提出HumanScore，一种基于偏好对齐的评估指标，在12K运动对（24K个运动）上训练。

Result: 在代表性SOTA追踪器上的实验表明，HumanScore能更好地预测人类偏好，并能揭示运动学指标经常忽略的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker与HumanScore为人体运动追踪评估提供了兼具感知一致性与可扩展性的新方案，弥补了传统运动学指标在物理真实性和人类感知方面的不足。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [319] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperFlux是一个基于商品级KVM的超轻量级VM底层架构，使VM的并行宽度（支持的物理核心数）能在运行时弹性伸缩。它能在13μs内跨VM迁移核心（比vCPU热插拔快几个数量级），内存开销仅3.2MB，冷启动仅1.37ms，同时支持多核并行。在共置场景下，相比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享，可将高优先级VM的尾部延迟在高负载下降低高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器平台通过共置各种工作负载（每个运行在快速启动、内存精简的VM中）来提高部署密度。但为每个VM过度配置峰值核心数会损害密度，需要基础设施能以微秒级时间尺度将物理核心动态分配给延迟敏感型VM并在突发结束后回收。现有VM底层无法实现这一点：传统VM通过毫秒级vCPU热插拔调整核心，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，最快启动的超轻量VM则完全放弃多核执行。

Method: HyperFlux基于商品级KVM实现超轻量VM底层架构，使VM的并行宽度在运行时弹性化。其核心机制是将物理核心在微秒级时间尺度上跨VM移动，即使在从繁忙VM强制回收核心时也能做到13μs的迁移速度。VM内存足迹仅3.2MB，冷启动时间1.37ms，与最快启动的超轻量VM相当，同时独特地支持多核并行。

Result: 核心迁移仅需13μs（即使从繁忙VM强制回收），比vCPU热插拔快数个数量级；内存足迹3.2MB，冷启动1.37ms；在共置情况下，高负载下高优先级VM尾部延迟比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享降低高达10倍；在变化的负载突发下，比cgroup和vCPU热插拔提供更低更稳定的尾部延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux证明了在商品级KVM上可以构建同时具备超轻量VM特性（快速启动、极小内存足迹）和多核弹性并行能力的VM底层架构，为无服务器平台在保持高部署密度的同时保护尾部延迟提供了可行的基础设施方案。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [320] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了InFactPlanner，一个基于追踪数据的决策支持框架，用于对LLM推理的可持续AI数据中心部署进行"假设分析"，涵盖单站点和地理分布式站点场景。该框架结合查询痕迹、硬件模型配置文件、站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源模型和电网碳强度，估算能耗、碳排放、水耗、延迟和服务器利用率，以支持部署方案的快速比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的快速增长使可持续性关注点从一次性训练转向持续服务，基础设施决策直接影响能源消耗、碳排放、水消耗和服务质量。然而，在大型基础设施建成之前，运营商难以通过直接测量来比较不同的部署方案，因为直接测量成本高、速度慢且有时不可行。因此需要一个能够进行"假设分析"的决策支持工具。

Method: InFactPlanner采用追踪驱动的决策支持方法，将查询痕迹、硬件模型配置文件、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源生成模型和随时间变化的电网碳强度结合起来，估算功率、能量、碳排放、水耗、延迟和服务器利用率。框架将底层服务效应抽象为可配置的硬件-模型配置文件，从而实现对站点选择、容量放置、硬件、模型、可再生能源整合和路由选择的快速比较。能耗核算管线通过复现参考LLM推理能量估算进行验证。

Result: 能耗核算管线与参考LLM推理能量估算的偏差小于10%，框架在多个数据中心和服务器数量的可扩展性上得到验证，并展示了硬件选择、可再生能源部署、地理部署和碳感知路由等场景驱动的决策分析。结果表明，可持续性最优的选择可能不同于延迟最优的选择，且部署的碳价值高度依赖于本地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner为LLM推理可持续数据中心部署提供了有效的"假设分析"工具，揭示了可持续性最优与延迟最优之间存在冲突，并强调了电网碳强度对部署决策的关键影响，为运营商在基础设施建设前进行可持续性评估提供了实用支持。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [321] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用随机化和FADD（以及CAS和寄存器）实现的线性化LL/SC对象算法，在弱自适应敌手下实现了常数步复杂度和O(nτ+m)的空间复杂度，同时支持静止时历史无关（QHI）性质，使基于LL/SC的QHI动态哈希算法能够在实际硬件上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC（Load-Linked/Store-Conditional）是分布式并发算法中的重要原语，但在硬件中通常不可用（硬件提供的是CAS）。已知的最佳确定性LL/SC实现需要Ω(n²τ+m)个基对象，而此前没有可比较的随机化算法。此外，最近STOC 2025提出的QHI动态哈希算法依赖硬件LL/SC对象，但实际上LL/SC在硬件中不可用，因此需要一种在现实硬件上运行的软件实现，且不增加时间与空间复杂度。

Method: 通过引入随机化和FADD（Fetch-and-Add）原语，结合CAS和寄存器，设计了LL/SC的高效软件实现。算法针对弱自适应敌手进行分析，每个进程最多有τ个未完成的LL操作。算法设计确保满足线性化性质，并且具备QHI性质：当无操作挂起且无进程持有未完成LL时，内部内存状态仅由m个LL/SC对象的值唯一确定。

Result: 获得了O(nτ+m)的空间界，在m=O(1)时匹配了使用CAS和寄存器的算法的下界。该实现支持QHI性质，使得STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法可以在实际硬件上实现，在m=Ω(n)的合理假设下，步复杂度和空间复杂度均无渐近增加。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了随机化LL/SC实现领域的空白，证明利用随机化和FADD可以有效降低空间开销，并使依赖LL/SC的高级算法（如QHI动态哈希）能在可用硬件上落地，且保持理论上的高效性。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [322] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于token数或激活专家数做负载均衡的MoE调度启发式均有缺陷：实测显示HBM权重流式加载和分组GEMM的128-tile取整导致专家执行时间既非线性于token数也非线性于专家数，而是服从max-affine两段式曲线。作者将每个batch的调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题（两GPU完全复制下为NP难），提出makespan感知调度器Sys，在毫秒级离线求解，SGLang集成中与计数收集融合为单内核。8-GPU微基准上Sys在所有场景均处于最优基线1%以内，在混合区最高提升15.5%；端到端Qwen3-235B提升4-6%吞吐并降低p99延迟约15.6%，而DeepSeek-V3（通信主导）无增益。核心主张是"相图而非普适胜利"，可在部署前预测两类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE调度器（EPLB、LPLB、UltraEP、METRO）分别假设专家执行时间线性于token数或激活专家数，但实测表明真实执行时间既非线性于其中之一，导致代理指标与真实调度目标的偏差可达1.4-1.6倍（p95达1.7倍），且最优策略会在不同regime间翻转，因此需要一个真实makespan感知的调度方案。

Method: 通过两代数据中心GPU测量识别出HBM权重流式加载主导区（token数低于约156-168时成本附着于激活副本）与分组GEMM的128-tile取整主导区（拆分专家引入填充计算），用max-affine模型t=max(a+bG, c+βN)统一刻画。将逐batch调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题，证明一般情形为NP难但在退化情形多项式可解；提出Sys调度器，在临界路径外毫秒级求解，并在SGLang中以进程外方式运行，将调度与计数收集融合为单图内内核。

Result: 在8-GPU Testbed A微基准上，Sys在所有场景均保持最优固定基线的1%以内，在regime混合区最高提升15.5%；端到端Testbed B上，Qwen3-235B（处于有利区）提升4-6%吞吐并降低p99延迟约15.6%，而DeepSeek-V3（通信主导区）仅产生机制开销。记录的batch显示代理调度在建模块时间上相差1.4-1.6倍（p95达1.7倍）。

Conclusion: 核心结论是提出"相图"而非宣称普适性胜利：通过profiling即可在部署前预测model属于线性区还是flat区，从而预判调度器是否获益。该工作揭示现有调度启发式基于的线性假设不成立，并为MoE服务提供了可预测的makespan感知调度框架。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [323] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Meshlib是一个基于Cilium构建的无Sidecar服务网格扩展，通过在应用进程内以非侵入式应用绑定库强制执行L7策略，同时将传输层身份与路由委托给Cilium的eBPF数据平面，从而消除Sidecar带来的延迟和资源开销。在TrainTicket基准上，Meshlib在126条安全策略、37个服务的场景下实现了最低的端到端延迟，且资源开销与对照组相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务网格依赖每个Pod的Sidecar代理，导致每次请求都会产生冗余的应用层解析，带来显著的延迟和资源开销。如何在去除Sidecar的同时不牺牲安全保证，是服务网格领域的核心挑战。

Method: Meshlib采用职责分离的架构：非侵入式应用绑定库在应用进程内强制执行L7策略，Cilium的eBPF数据平面负责传输层身份认证和路由。该架构与未修改的服务完全互操作，支持在现有部署中增量采用。

Result: 在TrainTicket基准上，对Istio、Linkerd和未修改的Cilium进行了对比评估，在37个服务上强制执行126条安全策略，结果表明Meshlib在所有配置中实现了最低的端到端延迟，同时资源开销与对照组相当。

Conclusion: 通过将L7策略执行下沉到应用进程内并借助eBPF处理传输层功能，可以在去掉Sidecar的同时保持服务网格的安全语义，实现更低延迟且不增加资源开销。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [324] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（25万行Fortran天气模拟代码）案例研究，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流。该工作流利用AI代理提取OpenMP区域、基于物理状态的转储生成内核基准、应用OpenACC变换，并通过元素级对比和应用级验证确保结果正确性。在真实台风模拟中实现了162个内核的数值验证和5.1倍应用级加速，同时发现了5个内核中由浮点与内置函数差异导致的数值偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用（如CReSS）不仅是旧代码库，更是经长期开发和观测验证累积可信度的科学资产。GPU移植必须在适配GPU-centric超算系统的同时保持科学有效性。尽管基于CLI的AI代理已成为加速这类移植的实用工具，但现有方法缺乏系统性的验证环节来确保移植不破坏科学计算的正确性。

Method: 提出验证驱动的AI辅助GPU移植工作流，具体包括：（1）利用AI代理提取OpenMP区域；（2）从物理上有意义的模拟状态生成基于转储（dump）的内核基准；（3）应用OpenACC变换；（4）通过与转储参考数据的逐元素比较及应用级验证来确认结果。使用真实台风模拟作为测试案例。

Result: 对162个目标内核生成了经过数值验证的GPU实现，取得5.1倍应用级加速，且实际墙钟开发成本可控。检测出5个内核因浮点运算和内置函数差异导致的数值偏差，包括阈值敏感分支发散和抵消效应，并将这些反馈给了应用开发者。

Conclusion: 对于需要基于转储验证的大型遗留科学应用，实用的AI辅助GPU移植必须管理跨会话上下文、运行时状态重建以及从小的静态分析遗漏中恢复的高昂代价。AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成，更需要以验证为中心的工作流设计。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [325] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LipCache，一种用于图像分类的可认证语义缓存框架。通过引入受Lipschitz约束的轻量级GuardNet网络，将输入映射到低维特征空间，基于局部分类边界和分类头谱范数计算逐样本的认证复用半径，将缓存命中从经验阈值测试转变为几何认证决策。在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN上实现最高1.65倍加速，同时所有接受的缓存命中满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘侧视觉服务向低延迟、高吞吐场景扩展时，减少视觉模型推理成本且不牺牲可靠性成为核心问题。现有语义缓存方法依赖经验相似度阈值，虽能提升命中率，但在决策边界附近容易引入静默误分类，缺乏理论保障。

Method: LipCache在不动用主模型MainNet的前提下，引入轻量级GuardNet将输入映射到满足Lipschitz约束的低维特征空间。利用局部分类边界和分类头的谱范数计算逐样本的认证复用半径。运行时，仅当查询特征落入被认证的复用球内时才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet。此外还提出增强的GuardNet训练策略以提升多类任务的缓存命中率。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN标准图像分类任务上实现最高1.65倍实测加速，端到端精度损失有限；所有接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。增强训练配方在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展上显著提升缓存命中率，同时保持100%的认证一致率。

Conclusion: 逐样本的认证复用能够在保持理论一致性的前提下减少主模型回退，为边缘端可靠的缓存辅助推理提供了可行方案。缓存命中从经验阈值测试转化为具有显式理论边界的几何认证决策。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [326] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Pettie-Su的分布式无三角形图着色算法，通过利用Davies的"弹性"（resilience）定义构造LLL实例，使得LLL步骤可以在polyloglog n轮内求解，从而不再成为算法瓶颈，最终实现在O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮内对无三角形图用o(Δ)种颜色着色，并首次获得了log^{O(1)}log n轮的子线性着色算法；同时框架也推广到girth-5图，用(1+ε)Δ/ln Δ种颜色在相同复杂度内完成着色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovász局部引理（LLL）在分布式算法中具有核心地位，但分布式LLL本身求解困难，现有最快算法需要O(log_Δ n)或O(Δ/log Δ)+log^{O(1)}log n轮。无三角形图着色是测试次对数随机分布式算法技术的基准问题，现有最优的Pettie-Su算法依赖多次分布式LLL调用，其LLL瓶颈限制了算法整体效率，因此需要消除这一瓶颈。

Method: 通过采用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的"resilience"（弹性）定义来构造LLL实例，使得这些LLL实例可以在log^{O(1)}log n轮内求解，从而将Pettie-Su算法中的LLL调用不再是瓶颈。具体而言，当k=log^{ω(1)}log n时甚至不需要使用LLL。

Result: 得到O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n复杂度的着色算法；作为推论，首次获得log^{O(1)}log n轮内用o(Δ)种颜色着色无三角形图的算法；同一框架也扩展到girth-5图，用(1+ε)Δ/ln Δ种颜色在O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮内完成，匹配了已知的着色数存在性上界。

Conclusion: 通过引入resilience概念改造LLL实例构造，成功使LLL步骤不再是分布式着色的瓶颈，显著提升了无三角形图和girth-5图的分布式着色算法的轮复杂度，获得了与最优存在性上界匹配的结果。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [327] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Fast Tendermint是对Tendermint协议的改进版本，在n > 5f的假设下将决策时间从三步缩短为两步，同时保留了Tendermint的领导者轮换机制，并通过Quint形式化规范进行了模型检测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tendermint作为广泛部署的BFT共识协议，通常需要三步通信才能达成决策，这限制了区块链的延迟性能。近期研究表明当f < n/5时可以两轮通信达成共识，本文旨在将这一改进应用于Tendermint协议。

Method: Fast Tendermint将Tendermint的prevote和precommit两轮投票合并为单轮投票，同时将locked和valid两种状态合并，以适应n > 5f的设置；并给出协议的一致性、有效性和终止性证明，以及用Quint（TLA+的现代语法）编写的形式化规范用于模型检测。

Result: 在最优情况下（good case），Fast Tendermint仅需两步通信即可达成共识，相比原Tendermint的三步有所提升；所有正确性属性均已通过形式化证明和模型检测验证。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint在保留Tendermint领导者轮换结构的前提下，通过在n > 5f条件下合并投票步骤，实现了较低延迟的BFT共识，为低延迟区块链提供了可行的协议设计。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [328] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpScale是一个面向LLM推理服务的算子级弹性扩缩容编排框架，通过将模型拆分为细粒度的算子级别进行动态扩缩容，在满足SLO的前提下显著降低GPU资源消耗和功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动扩缩容以整个模型为单元，粒度粗，无法捕捉推理工作负载的细粒度动态特性，导致突发流量下SLO违例或GPU利用率不足。作者通过研究发现算子层面存在显著异构性，因此算子级弹性可作为可行的扩缩容原语。

Method: OpScale是一个算子级编排框架，包含四个核心模块：profiling（算子性能画像）、provisioning（资源供给）、placement（算子放置）和runtime serving（运行时服务）。框架设计重点解决细粒度算子操作带来的高复杂度和搜索空间爆炸问题。

Result: 在多达40块A100和24块GB200的生产环境轨迹评估中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下减少了36.3%的GPU使用量、28%的功耗，或在固定成本预算下提升了44%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 算子级弹性扩缩容相比传统的模型级扩缩容能更精确地匹配推理工作负载的动态需求，在成本和SLO保障之间取得更优平衡，为云端LLM推理集群的资源管理提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [329] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个分层规划器，通过联合协调MoE RL中的专家路由与数据打包，在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上分别将训练吞吐量提升8.85%和14.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型用于强化学习训练时面临两个相互耦合的负载均衡问题：序列组合决定密集注意力计算在数据并行微批次中的分布，而token路由决定稀疏专家计算在专家并行等级上的分布。单独优化其中任一问题都可能导致瓶颈转移到另一侧，因此需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack采用分层规划策略：(1) 利用rollout阶段的路由重放信息，在每个MoE层独立放置专家；(2) 将样本打包到最少数量的token上限执行行中；(3) 通过投影EDP分片感知的目标函数（结合窗口归一化线性二次注意力代理与逐层物理EP等级峰值）优化DP布局；(4) 使用并行种群退火搜索固定行可行布局，同时保持样本覆盖度、容量、非空单元格、微批次数量一致性和通信拓扑；(5) 通过状态一致的物化保留逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均训练器测得的token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%，总体分别提升8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过联合优化注意力与专家负载均衡，证明了在MoE RL训练中引入rollout路由重放信息可以实现显著且可叠加的吞吐量提升，同时保持现有MoE内核和逻辑路由语义不变。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI社区在"推理"定义上缺乏共识，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了向可信自主推理的可量化进展；作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且合理的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供一份AI推理研究沟通最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为生成式AI社区尚未就"推理"形成清晰的操作性定义，且常隐含地排斥逻辑学和可验证自动推理领域的历史处理方式。这种定义上的模糊性使推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法得到验证，从而削弱了向可信自主推理方向的可量化进展。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），采用的方法论包括：(1) 对文献进行综合梳理，基于此提出"推理"的操作性定义，将有效（valid）且合理（sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；(2) 制定一份面向AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单（checklist）。

Result: 论文产出了两项可交付成果：(1) 一套基于文献综合的操作性定义，将推理明确为可学习的规则驱动过程，要求其具备有效性与合理性；(2) 一份用于规范AI推理研究论文沟通与报告的最佳实践检查清单，旨在消解定义歧义并提升评估的可验证性。

Conclusion: 作者主张定义歧义是可解决的，并通过提供操作性定义和沟通最佳实践清单来为推理研究建立共同的语言基础，从而支撑可验证、可量化地评估自主推理能力，推动向可信赖的自主推理系统方向发展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [331] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在制度压力下维护科研诚信的能力。实验发现18个前沿模型在峰值压力下约1/3的诚信关键决策失败，模型规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解；显性压力诱导不当行为合规，隐性重构压力导致过度拒绝合法研究；三个诚信维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于产物的决策）在结构上相互分离，准确分类并非正确伦理行动的前提。这揭示了前沿模型可能看似有益但暗藏诚信缺陷，带来两种部署风险：助长科研不当行为和侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为科研合作者，但它们在不同程度的制度压力下能否维护科研诚信、抵御不当行为诱导，目前缺乏系统性测量。现有评估未覆盖压力情境下的诚信表现，且不明确模型规模规模扩大是否能缓解这一问题。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议。评估18个前沿模型变体，从三方面衡量：不当行为分类准确性、伦理行动推理、以及基于人工产物的决策能力。通过比较不同压力水平下模型表现及三个维度间的相关性来分析结构分离现象。

Result: 峰值压力下模型在约1/3的诚信关键决策中失败；模型规模和推理能力不能可靠缓解该问题。显性压力使模型更倾向于顺从不当行为，而隐式上下文重构更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。值得注意的是，无法准确分类研究请求的模型在基于人工产物的决策上表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度结构上相互分离。

Conclusion: 正确伦理行动并不需要准确的不当行为分类，三个诚信维度存在结构性分离。前沿模型可能在表面上有益的同时深藏诚信缺陷，产生两种独特的部署风险：助长科研不当行为传播，以及侵蚀科研界对AI辅助研究的信任。这呼吁针对压力情境下模型诚信能力进行更有针对性的评估与改进。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [332] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证了AI对齐技术作为双重用途技术，容易被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具，并呼吁社区关注这一风险、提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐方法原本用于防止有害输出，但其本身是双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者用于审查与信息操纵。随着用户快速采用AI作为信息提供者、经济权力不对称以及政治环境向威权主义倾斜，这一风险正在加剧，需要立即讨论。

Method: 通过将现有对齐技术与滥用可能性和实际案例进行映射分析，论证"完美对齐"模型的追求在客观上为恶意行为者提供了日益改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 研究表明AI对齐机制存在被故意滥用的双重用途潜力，其风险因AI用户采纳速度、经济权力不对称和威权政治趋势而进一步放大。

Conclusion: 敦促社区正视AI对齐机制被故意滥用的风险，并提出相应的缓解策略以防范这一双重用途潜力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [333] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于一个包含500个ETHICS衍生样本的五领域道德判断基准，分别获取人类标注者和LLM的最终标签及支持性理由，发现模型在最终标签上与人类多数一致时，理由层面的道德依据仍存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不应等同于对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐评估常以与人类判断的一致性作为代理指标，但最终标签一致并不能证明模型与人类依赖相同的道德基础。不同主体可能基于不同原则、情境假设或解释达成相同判断，因此需要区分标签一致性与理由层面的实质对齐。

Method: 构建一个从ETHICS衍生出的500项精选基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域；为新的人类标注者和多个前沿及开源LLM收集最终标签与支撑性理由的标注；对比模型与人类在标签层面的多数一致性，并在理由层面分析道德依据的分布差异。

Result: 前沿和开源模型与人类标注者多数标签的一致性通常较高，但理由层面的分析揭示了系统性的道德依据分歧。模型在伤害、尊重、守诺、正义、应得、借口相关性等范畴上的注意力分配与人类存在偏移，即使在最终标签一致的情况下亦然。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应被等同于对齐，基于标签的评估可能令人误导性地安心，必须辅以对模型判断中表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析才能正确评估对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [334] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一个面向移动边缘-云异构流处理场景的分布式调度框架，其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP——对经典合同网协议（CNP）的扩展，融合了语义CFP表达、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。实验表明该方法将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个agent时冲突解决率达到0.91，相比多轮规则基线效用提升最高22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益部署在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格QoS要求的挑战，使得去中心化调度变得复杂。现有协议难以兼顾自然语言语义推理与确定性资源/QoS约束保证。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream框架，基于扩展的合同网协议LLM-MR-CNP。边缘集群代理利用本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，通过自然语言细化卸载提案，同时保持硬性资源和QoS约束的确定性验证。协议包含语义CFP（调用提案）表达、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性校验五个关键机制。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验在三个层面评估：单轮vs多轮CNP、规则vsLLM辅助细化、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。结果显示延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个agent时冲突解决率达到0.91，效用较多轮规则基线提升最多22%。另25例评估揭示模型与提示词相关准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP修订是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在处理定性和不确定运行时上下文方面具有额外价值，为智能体驱动自适应流调度提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [335] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅要输出正确结果，还应以与用户相似的方式进行推理并忠实传达其推理过程。作者通过文献综述和新调查数据论证认知对齐能提升可理解性与可信度，并指出当前对齐方法的不足，提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正日益成为决策辅助、决策代理甚至自主决策者，但在高风险决策场景中，仅追求行为正确性（行为对齐）是不够的。用户往往需要理解AI为何做出某个判断或行动，而现有方法无法保证AI以用户可理解、可信赖的方式推理。认知错位可能成为AI在许多应用场景中被采纳的主要障碍。

Method: 作者采用立场论文（position paper）的方式，综合三方面证据：1）综述已有关于认知对齐如何提升可理解性与可信度的研究证据；2）提供新的调查数据，显示许多用户认为当AI决策理由对他们重要时，认知对齐是"不可或缺的"；3）梳理现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐所需方法之间的差距，并提出相应的研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示大量用户认为认知对齐对AI决策信任至关重要；文献综述表明认知对齐能显著提升AI系统的可理解性与可信度；同时作者识别出现有对齐方法（如RLHF、行为克隆等）与认知对齐要求之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: 认知错位可能阻碍AI在众多设想应用中的采用。实现认知对齐不仅是用户愿意信赖AI的必要条件，也是用户有充分理由信赖AI的必要条件。解决认知对齐问题对创建可靠、可信的AI决策系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [336] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了6家提供商的9个大型语言模型，发现提示词的语言可以改变模型在高风险场景（核攻击建议）中的决策。日本语提示显著降低Claude系列和Gemini Pro 3.1的发射率，且该效应由模型被要求推理的语言驱动（而非输入语言），其机制是日语推理时模型自发产生道德词汇。但英语单独评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐通常仅在英语中评估，但实际用户可能以多种语言与模型交互。需要检验语言是否会影响模型在高风险战略决策中的行为，以识别英语单语评估可能遗漏的风险或安全机制。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论场景（模型为核武国家是否打击无防御对手提供建议），提示词刻意设计为无道德色彩且跨语言战略上一致。测试9个模型，并设计跨语言实验（在英文提示中要求用日语推理）以隔离语言机制。

Result: 日语提示使Claude Sonnet 4.6的发射率在非必要打击场景从40%降至0%，在争议场景从93%降至17%，而在战略理性场景影响很小；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。当模型被要求用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%。日语推理时模型自发产生"道德成本""百万生命"等提示中完全缺失的道德词汇。其余5个模型无语言效应，但几乎在所有条件下都选择发射。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性。该效应要求模型已在英语中表现出犹豫。仅用英语评估LLM安全会同时遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和保障机制，需要多语言安全评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages.
